===============================================================================
File: match output.txt

Description:
    Log file generated during automatic verification of the English movie script
    dataset using the OMDb API.

Purpose:
    As part of the dataset construction process for the paper
    "Hierarchical Ordinal Framework for Automated Movie Censorship Using
    Full-Length Scripts" (IEEE GCAT 2025), movie titles, release years, and
    MPAA ratings encoded in dataset filenames were validated against metadata
    retrieved from the Open Movie Database (OMDb). This log records the outcome
    of each verification.

Verification Process:
    1. Parse filename into rating, title, and release year.
    2. Query the OMDb API.
    3. Compare returned metadata with the expected values.
    4. Report successful matches, mismatches, and failed lookups.

Log Indicators:
    ✅ Perfect match
        Movie title, release year, and MPAA rating matched.

    ⚠ Metadata mismatch
        Movie found, but one or more metadata fields differed.

    ❌ Failed
        Movie could not be verified after multiple API attempts.

Purpose of this log:
    - Dataset quality assurance
    - Metadata validation
    - Reproducibility of the dataset curation process

Author:
    Pratik N. Kalamkar
===============================================================================
